# B0246 — Shared plans and particular pleasures

These three Event families examine how a proposed shared good becomes somebody's actual work, leisure or pleasure. Ayumu and Madoka's cafe tests the distance between a giver's service ideal and the recipient's own mixed experience. Haru, Rona, Hime and Momoka negotiate a camp whose practical constraints can be imaginatively recombined without erasing disagreement. Miu, Mimi and Kazumi develop an appreciation party through different associations and contributions, then explicitly refuse a single-origin account. The endings do not all have the same evidential status: a cafe and party are reported complete, whereas this camp prehistory ends at a lodging candidate and anticipation.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0246-001 — The causal hinge is Player concern about one-person work**

The causal hinge is Player concern about one-person work. Ayumu answers that practical concern by identifying a partner, but an identified candidate is not yet a consenting collaborator. Madoka moves from interested observer to personally implicated subject; the closing surprise makes that uncompleted transition visible.

#### Factual reconstruction

- P1-7: Player asks for uniform opinions. Madoka proposes short sleeves for the summer beach and also expresses a wish for frills. Musubi agrees about cool clothing and raises heatstroke concern, then recognizes the desire for a cute outfit. Ayumu presents cuteness as necessary to poekyun. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0154.json__e386d1ce16578bde?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0154.json__e386d1ce16578bde?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`.
- P8-15: Ayumu proposes a swimwear motif and a cool, soft-looking service uniform. Player notes the beach setting. Madoka explicitly objects that the proposed uniform is too embarrassing for her. Musubi supports her and rejects too much exposure, even after Ayumu argues for its appeal. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0154.json__e386d1ce16578bde?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0154.json__e386d1ce16578bde?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`.
- P16-22: Ayumu describes an imagined Madoka apology in that proposed outfit. Musubi next has an ellipsis utterance. Player calls the idea good; Musubi then calls swimwear more functional at the beach. Madoka questions both responses. The chapter ends without an actual garment, final design, agreement or completed costume change. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0154.json__e386d1ce16578bde?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0154.json__e386d1ce16578bde?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`.

### Chapter5 — 504015205

Complete P0-41, 39written/3command-only/32dialogue voice pages. Original full freeze 2026-11-04T06:11:27.868249+00:00, SHA256 6cd64ff10233529981161334621e27142efb61ed371e62d29ccd75b4347af49c.

#### Factual reconstruction

- P1-10: In the office-framed celebration, Player toasts the cafe success. Musubi reports large attendance and Coney pleasure at Nanasta publicity; Player reports a spontaneous live. Musubi qualifies the celebration by saying she only went to check and was pulled into it by Coney. Player redirects toward thanking Ayumu and Madoka; both credit collective effort. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0156.json__ba359676fb66c3a1?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0156.json__ba359676fb66c3a1?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`.
- P11-17: Ayumu wants a bigger event next year. Musubi names Ayumu insistence on costume fabrics and menu ingredients; Ayumu declares no compromise in her maid practice. Player says those expenses left the result roughly break-even and Coney disappointed. Musubi quotes Coney calling the unforeseen result a major miscalculation. No itemized accounting, amount or formal next-year cancellation is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0156.json__ba359676fb66c3a1?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0156.json__ba359676fb66c3a1?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`.
- P18-29: Ayumu calls the work enjoyable. Musubi anticipates a different judgment from Madoka, who instead gives her own mixed account: the uniform was embarrassing and speaking to many people difficult, but working with Ayumu was perhaps quite enjoyable, correcting an initial little to quite. Player attributes customer pleasure to that enjoyment; Madoka asks him not to praise her so much. Ayumu defines poekyun through happiness in both giving and receiving service. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0156.json__ba359676fb66c3a1?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0156.json__ba359676fb66c3a1?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`.
- P30-41: Ayumu invites Madoka to her existing workplace and predicts top-maid potential. Madoka expresses surprise and calls on Player and Musubi. Player considers repeated access to the apology appealing; Musubi imagines obtaining the shop point card if visiting. Madoka calls to both again while Ayumu repeats the invitation. No acceptance, employment arrangement or actual future visit is shown. The final two command pages have the same unresolved voice reference and the last native subtitle says end. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0156.json__ba359676fb66c3a1?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0156.json__ba359676fb66c3a1?doc=primary&log=41#/Pages/41`.

The public cafe and spontaneous live enter this chapter as retrospective reports. The office backdrop cannot be turned into proof of the cafe location or a live performance capture.

Success separates into publicity, participant experience and monetary result. The reported large turnout and positive promotion coexist with reported break-even finances. Ayumu explicit two-sided service ideal gives poekyun a substantive relational meaning beyond a cute manner. The interpretation is not exhausted by a superficial persona or consumer appeal. Its local promise is tested against Madoka account, unresolved work conditions and the closing invitation rather than treated as a validated universal system.

**CL-B0246-028 — Musubi actual complaint about being pulled into the event limits the opening celebration, while Player redirection leaves that complaint without a substantive response here**

Musubi actual complaint about being pulled into the event limits the opening celebration, while Player redirection leaves that complaint without a substantive response here. Her earlier refusal and differentiated help are not silently rewritten as full prior agreement. The source remains her report; it supplies no witnessed physical severity or complete production account.

**CL-B0246-029 — The ending reopens the difference between a person enjoyment and others plans for that person**

The ending reopens the difference between a person enjoyment and others plans for that person. Ayumu invitation and optimistic forecast are not Madoka decision. Player and Musubi respond through imagined customer access when Madoka calls to them. The comic repetition qualifies a total care/repair closure while leaving real collaboration and enjoyment intact.

**CL-B0246-030 — Player customer-pleasure explanation and Musubi broad comment that Ayumu makes everything poekyun are attributed appraisals, not independent access to every customer mind or a universal character rule**

Player customer-pleasure explanation and Musubi broad comment that Ayumu makes everything poekyun are attributed appraisals, not independent access to every customer mind or a universal character rule. Similarly, a future top-maid forecast supplies no achieved competence, rank or readiness promotion.

### Complete-family causal and epistemic reconstruction

#### Factual reconstruction

- Chapter1 introduces a cafe proposal through Player report of Coney document and self-praise. Ayumu offers relevant expertise and volunteers, Player raises solo staffing, Musubi refuses another promotional role, and Ayumu names a surprised Madoka as partner before her agreement is shown.
- Chapter2 presents an office-framed service exercise. Musubi supplies a procedural prompt, Ayumu corrects repetition/greeting norms and proposes a cute apology for errors, Player questions that universal reassurance, and Madoka agrees to try the immediate exercise. Musubi registers a local effect while Madoka remains explicitly embarrassed.
- Chapter3 solicits uniform opinions. Madoka gives heat-related and aesthetic preferences, then objects to Ayumu different proposal. Musubi first supports the objection, then expresses a functional rationale after Ayumu imagined service appeal and Player approval. The chosen design and agreement remain unseen.
- Chapter4 reports training difficulty despite Madoka effort. Musubi uses Ayumu learning history to propose practical imitation; the later observed exercise includes speech-style copying. Madoka expresses embarrassment inside that new register. Ayumu announces a tomorrow opening; actual practical mastery is not shown.
- Chapter5 gives a retrospective celebration: attendance/publicity and a spontaneous live are reported; Musubi also reports unwanted involvement. Player reports roughly break-even finances after material expenses. Madoka supplies her own mixed account of difficult/embarrassing work and enjoyment with Ayumu. Ayumu states a two-sided service ideal, then invites her to another workplace; Madoka future choice remains unresolved.

#### Causal structure

- The solo-work concern produces the named-partner solution, but selection precedes demonstrated assent. Subsequent participation is evidenced by actual narrow attempts and replies, without inventing the omitted initial negotiation.
- Novice practice exposes both procedural gaps and difficulty meeting an expressive service norm. Encouragement can enable a local attempt while recipient approval and performer comfort remain different outcomes.
- The uniform dispute tests the limits of shared cuteness criteria. Ayumu projection of audience appeal changes expressed alignments, but exact private causes are unresolved and Madoka objection is not withdrawn on screen.
- A learning-history question yields a practical modeling proposal; its implementation extends into linguistic persona. Actual register uptake does not establish achieved practical service competence, universal pedagogy or elimination of embarrassment.
- The announced opening is followed by retrospective work/event reports. Source-explicit continuity supports that bounded sequence; the public service and live themselves are not depicted. Neither the chapter numbers nor menu priority supply an exact calendar.
- Final appraisals separate publicity, money and participant experience. Madoka enjoyment gives a substantive response to Ayumu ideal without erasing costs. The renewed invitation repeats the unresolved question of whether others desired service role will become the participant own choice.

#### Epistemic boundaries

- Player report and quotation mediate Coney initial proposal; Musubi and Player later mediate Coney publicity pleasure, expense disappointment and alleged drawing-in of Musubi. No Coney native speaker/portrait or performed delivery is supplied in these chapters.
- Musubi protective expectations, Ayumu lost-smile report, Madoka own embarrassment/enjoyment and Player customer-response explanation are different evidence scopes. Madoka direct self-evaluation is not replaced by another character guess.
- Cafe attendance, publicity, spontaneous live and financial result are attributed retrospective reports, with no raw customer testimony, observed stage performance or independently audited accounting. Successful publicity and break-even result are compatible measures.
- Ayumu universal cute-apology assurance, Musubi trend forecast, Ayumu next-year ambition/top-maid prediction and Player/Musubi imagined future visits remain claims/proposals; no future outcome is imported backward.
- The former B0244 Musubi announcement-versus-completed-departure qualification remains current. Here too a refusal, proposal, report, present attempt and completed event are distinguished; static fades or blank slots are not proof of physical movement.
- No semantic source from the next two Event families or2053 has been exposed to the analyst before this family freeze. All15 labels/layers/counts were already metadata-exposed, and automatic complete-packet preparation was separately recorded after the genuine prospective gates.

#### Character relations

- Ayumu expertise and generosity are real situated resources; her admired service practice is not merely a false persona. It also supplies a model she extends to another person preferences and future, requiring recipient qualification rather than universal endorsement.
- Madoka asks, learns, volunteers specific efforts, voices aesthetic preferences and objections, and claims mixed enjoyment. Her own agency coexists with nervousness, service difficulty and renewed appeals; neither total helplessness nor a complete cure is supported.
- Musubi gives practical help, local solidarity and recognition, while later alignments and imagined customer access qualify a total protector model. Her reported unwanted live participation remains part of the event account.
- Player raises staffing, seeks opinions, thanks labor and reports financial constraints, but also appraises appealing service, deflects a complaint and imagines recurring access when Madoka appeals. Those are source-local tensions, not a timeless total managerial verdict.
- Coney is known here through reported planning/publicity/cost responses and Musubi participation complaint. The Nanasta publicity objective is explicit; the cafe name777 alone does not establish777SISTERS unit membership, a new institutional lineup or a new stable group.
- Ayumu two-sided service ideal supplies a supported interpretive center: pleasure in giving and receiving can coexist, but cannot be presumed for every role or override expressed limits. Madoka work-with-Ayumu enjoyment is a particular corroborating case with preserved material and emotional costs.

#### Literary synthesis with retained alternatives

**CL-B0246-031 — Service happiness requires the participant own account**

Ayumu's ideal is a substantive relation between giving and receiving pleasure, not merely a mask to expose. Madoka's enjoyment of working with her provides particular support. Yet Madoka also retains difficulty and embarrassment, and the initial preselection and renewed job invitation do not supply missing assent. A simple coercion-only reading loses Madoka's positive testimony; an uncomplicated success narrative loses her expressed limits and the unfinished future choice.

### Complete-family causal and epistemic reconstruction

#### Factual reconstruction

- Chapter1: Four Witch members discuss an imminent camp after delegating accommodation to Player. His reassurance is quoted against Momoka contrasting recollection and Haru qualified trust. Practice, curry preparation and leisure suggestions overlap; Rona also wants play. Momoka claims a competence-proving plan, withholds it and urges a sudden outing.
- Chapter2: The destination is an arcade. Momoka presents rhythm play as training while explicitly hoping to avoid lessons; peers recognize her game ability, but Hime denies the exemption. Haru initiates dance play and all three ask to join Momoka. Repeated shared rounds leave her exhausted; proposed stamina/basic work expands, Haru also admits fatigue and Rona proposes tea.
- Chapter3: The next scene uses a shopping-mall backdrop and textual enjoyment of sweets/doughnuts, not a pictured cafe interior. Food and activities remain candidates. Momoka recognizes Rona wish for flowing noodles; preparation effort and space arise, and Rona challenges an assumption that absent Player will be free to do the work. Hime changes a hot tofu offer to cold tofu after Momoka notices the seasonal contradiction. Further shopping is proposed despite Momoka wish to go home.
- Chapter4: Members report shopping and use received lottery tickets for five prize-bearing draws, including a redo of Rona empty attempt. Three tissue outcomes, Hime snack and last-draw fourth-prize fireworks occur. Hime shares the snack choice with Momoka and accepts the final draw under a no-blame condition. The first-prize four-person hot spring remains unachieved. Haru proposes camp-night fireworks, then returning to Nanasta.
- Chapter5: Return is explicitly greeted. Player presents a rural accommodation candidate and describes budget/date constraints and new-song costume costs, not a booking transaction. His low-pay account blocks Momoka private-funds suggestion. Haru connects the acquired fireworks, proposed noodles and practice space to the place; Player surprise marks local reception of the noodle idea. Haru/Rona explicitly assent, Hime responds positively and Rona retains some fear. Momoka still resists running. The family ends with laughter and an unresolved command-only closing cue, before any actual camp.

#### Causal structure

- An effort to escape formal lessons creates a socially useful demonstration, then repeated shared activity and more proposed training. The reversal preserves Momoka recognized competence and the unequal number of joined rounds; it is not proof that she is simply deficient or that the others are inexhaustible.
- Planning develops through revision and incidental opportunity. The shopping proposed in Chapter3 is newly supported by Chapter4 purchase reports; its lottery yields actual fireworks that Haru later includes in the accommodation discussion. These explicit continuities differ from assuming all desired activities happened.
- The final venue reframing reuses independently accumulated wishes: open practice, Rona flowing-noodle desire, an acquired leisure object and expected stars. Haru connects them into an attractive possibility without proving apparatus, weather, facility suitability, reservation or outcome.
- Ordinary labor and resource limits remain visible. Hime/Haru name preparation and space, Rona questions assumed manager idleness, and Player later states money/date constraints. Group pleasure has infrastructure; the story does not establish unlimited worker availability or an obligation to subsidize it personally.
- The lottery changes possible camp leisure through chance, while Hime gift and group appreciation give a modest win relational value. A prior-winner heuristic and later success do not prove a luck power, fair odds or moral desert.
- Affiliation does not require identical desire. Comic corrections circulate: Hime challenges lesson exemption and fraud allegations, Momoka notices the hotpot inconsistency and understands Rona wish, Haru revises expansive ideas, and Rona keeps proposals provisional. Closing laughter coexists with fear and exercise protest.

#### Epistemic boundaries

- Known-then freezes are preserved chapter by chapter. Chapter1 plan/destination is genuinely unknown until Chapter2; the earlier footstep/fade references are not retroactively treated as independently observed travel. Chapter2 tea proposal is followed by a new sweets/doughnuts scene, not proof that tea specifically occurred.
- Chapter3 shopping intent becomes later reported purchases; Chapter4 return intent becomes Chapter5 explicit greetings. Menu contents, actual cooking and the camp remain open. Later confirmation is recorded at its later source horizon rather than inserted into earlier states.
- Quoted manager confidence, Momoka strained-face recollection and Haru probably remain limited accounts. Chapter5 newly supplies Player search/candidate/constraint statements, but not a retrospective proof of booking at Chapter1.
- Player now hears flowing somen named and asks about it. That is local reception of a proposal, not proof that he knew the prior conversation, accepted construction labor or had spare capacity. Low pay and budget are his reports, not audited numbers.
- Ghost worry is fear, not world evidence that ghosts exist. Hime protection is bravado, Player reassurance qualified, and Rona acceptance does not erase fear. Connectivity absence, lottery fraud and permanent luck remain unverified claims/inferences.
- Player reference to a recent Shizuka island villa supplies an explicit relative-memory comparison. Catalog order and a prequel title do not yield a complete cross-event calendar or a uniquely dated causal sequence. Earlier published unit/Main histories retain their own horizons.
- WITCH NUMBER4/UNIT-0008 and777 Sisters/UNIT-0023 are distinct current formations; historical Seven Sisters/UNIT-0001 is separate. Four-member practice within a busy macro-unit context is not new group formation, conflict or readiness promotion.
- The unnamed lottery attendant remains document-local; no master identity or merge with other generic clerks. Literal collective/paired labels remain unexpanded into invented individual performers or new entities.

#### Character relations

- Haru: Brings practice and shared pleasure into one developing plan, initiates a shared game, acknowledges Momoka competence and her own fatigue, revises food ideas and reframes a constrained venue. Encouragement is real but forecasts do not establish execution, universal managerial competence or unanimous agreement.
- Rona: Advocates lessons and wants play, voices shared-food preferences and the flowing-noodle wish, questions presumed manager idleness, apologizes at an empty draw, proposes a break and explicitly accepts the venue while retaining fear. The local acts resist flattening her into pure helplessness or a cured opposite.
- Hime: Raises logistics/practice questions, recognizes game skill while refusing exemption, checks speculative accusations, shares a snack choice, conditionally accepts a last draw and offers protection/training banter. Her hotpot inconsistency can be corrected; local familiarity with an attendant is not a new biography.
- Momoka: Expresses play and rest desires, seeks a lesson exemption, demonstrates game skill, understands Rona hidden wish, notices inconsistent reasoning, responds to disappointment with unsupported suspicion and resists running. Contributions and costs coexist with avoidance; no single vice or completed competence redemption exhausts her.
- Player: First appears through others logistical accounts, then directly explains a candidate and limited resources, admits uncertainty, receives an unexpected activity idea and asks for acceptance. No confirmed reservation, workload audit, private subsidy or complete safety policy. Shizuka remains a named comparative reference, not newly present.
- The unit shares correction, generosity and anticipation without all positions converging. Haru/Rona explicit yes, Hime positive response, Momoka continuing objections and collective laughter are kept distinct; neither an all-member training contract nor dissolved affiliation follows.

#### Literary synthesis with retained alternatives

**CL-B0246-075 — The work/play dispute is reorganized through shared activity**

Momoka tries to make recognized rhythm-game competence count as an exemption from lessons. Hime grants the competence and denies the inference; Haru's dance-game suggestion turns that strategy into repeated group participation and fatigue. Rona wants lessons as well as play, and Haru acknowledges her own difficulty. The group is neither a pure disciplinary bloc confronting a lazy outsider nor proof that games have produced measured musical growth.

**CL-B0246-076 — Small recognition and revision make planning collective**

Momoka recognizes Rona's noodle wish; Hime revises a seasonal inconsistency and shares a snack choice; Haru turns a contingent fireworks win into a camp-night possibility. #### Factual reconstruction

- P1-4: Miu announces arrival at Old-EDO Street and explains choosing it through her association of autumn with Japanese style. Mimi asks why this place; Kazumi says many people here walk in Japanese clothing. This is a new actual scene after the previous outward-search proposal, not proof of a total calendar or a universally Japanese essence of autumn. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0163.json__a16ee412b97b84e5?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0163.json__a16ee412b97b84e5?doc=primary&log=4#/Pages/4`.
- P5-14: Mimi praises Japanese clothing and imagines a cute kimono suiting Miu. Miu describes occasional New Year kimono and festival yukata use, especially a short frilled style she and friends compare for cuteness. Kazumi questions whether that remains Japanese clothing; Mimi says Miu could make it work. No one actually changes clothes in the native/inspected scene. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0163.json__a16ee412b97b84e5?doc=primary&log=5#/Pages/5`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0163.json__a16ee412b97b84e5?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`.
- P15-24: Asked for an image, Kazumi supplies a romanticized autumn-festival vignette and associates its elegance with Mimi. Mimi instead describes inn relaxation after bathing, food/drink and table tennis, calling inn yukata the official uniform of hot-spring table tennis. These are contrasting evoked images/appraisals, not independently witnessed episodes or an external clothing rule. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0163.json__a16ee412b97b84e5?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0163.json__a16ee412b97b84e5?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`.
- P25-33: Miu proposes a group yukata table-tennis tournament and Mimi initially agrees. Kazumi notes that this is sport like the already rejected sports day; Miu acknowledges the point. Kazumi nevertheless likes the prospect of everyone wearing yukata. Mimi keeps shared wearing as one candidate, Miu anticipates it and Kazumi proposes continuing the search. No final whole-event plan or actual tournament is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0163.json__a16ee412b97b84e5?doc=primary&log=25#/Pages/25`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0163.json__a16ee412b97b84e5?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33`.
